FUNDED TRADES (25; only lots the shared allocator funded — refused shadow signals are in signals.csv; $100,000.00 per trade, never compounded)
  fills: the engine's raw execution prices; friction = 5 bps per side on each fill (commission $0); interest = 11% simple on $100,000.00 for the session-date days held (interestDays in the CSV);
  gross P&L = shares × (exit fill − entry fill) before costs; net = gross − friction − interest (the engine's net);
  MAE = the engine's worst minute mark while held (shares × last price − principal − accrued interest).

▶ P1 scenarios
  ✔ a sale due after the last event executes; the replacement is still reserved at the end (0.770876ms)

▶ stop policies
  ✔ net: inactive while a fill at the stop would lose after interest, active once above it, exits at the stop level (1.970965ms)
  ✔ gross breakeven (B-B) latches when the stop level reaches the entry price and holds through a loss-making sale (0.474584ms)
  ✔ gross threshold (B-A) activates at the configured appreciation, not before, and is a latch (0.526514ms)
  ✔ always (B-D) is active from the session after the entry, and a gap through it fills at the open (0.280082ms)
  ✔ two-stage (B-C): the protective stop acts in the EARLY stage, the trailing stop after the breakeven latch (0.301312ms)
  ✔ netLatched never switches off; net can (3.143373ms)
  ✔ replayPosition reproduces the engine exit of a given fill (0.199271ms)
  ✔ the maximum hold still sells a losing position whatever the policy, and the buffer only acts after it applies (0.478924ms)
✔ stop policies (8.03089ms)
▶ entry modes
  ✔ always: arms on the first eligible minute, buys on the rebound from the lowest later low, re-arms after an exit, never on the exit session (0.777366ms)
  ✔ swing: the rolling low excludes the current session, a close at or below it arms, the rebound buys (0.465344ms)
  ✔ C3 arbitration: an armed swing setup yields to the deep path on the minute D1 would arm, and the window stays open (0.349742ms)
  ✔ path causality: nothing arms before the eligibility session, and the rolling low never sees the current session (0.226341ms)
✔ entry modes (1.968964ms)

▶ entry-day activation in a book
  ✔ a same-session adverse move after the fill exits at L that session (next-session waits for the next open) (0.499144ms)
  ✔ a fill-minute low through L exits in the fill minute itself (stop fill) or at the next minute’s open (next-open) (0.264312ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a minute opening through L on the entry day loses more than the 8% distance (0.149221ms)
  ✔ is not a loss cap: a position the entry day did not stop gaps far through L the next morning (0.146691ms)

▶ the decline gate
  ✔ K = 1: blocks arming after a failed attempt, logs each refused session, and ends at the cooldown (0.658175ms)

  ✔ a profitable exit ends an unblocked episode, and the continuation re-entry is untouched (0.242052ms)
  ✔ never blocks a book without failures (the recovery path is unchanged) (0.314752ms)
